    29	
    30	/// Which tables of the project's schema a device holds offline: the README
    31	/// and init's summary say what works offline from this, never from the
    32	/// starter's own (synced) todos schema.
    33	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.6 — wave 7 (R3): "works offline" was printed beside online-only schemas.
    34	#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
    35	pub(super) enum Offline {
    36	    /// The schema does not compile (yet), or there is none: no claim either way.
    37	    Unknown,
    38	    /// The tables that declare `sync`, and how many tables there are (views excluded).
    39	    Tables { synced: Vec<String>, total: usize },
    40	}
    41	
    42	impl Offline {
    43	    /// The README paragraph (it always starts with "Offline", which is how
    44	    /// `init --adapt` finds it again), wrapped like the rest of the README.
    45	    pub(super) fn paragraph(&self, platform: Platform) -> String {
    46	        let lead = match platform { Platform::Web => "Offline:", Platform::Expo => "Offline, in device builds and on the web:" };
    47	        let body = match self {
    48	            Offline::Unknown => "tables that declare `sync` read and write offline and sync when the link returns; the others read from the server, and writes to them made offline are kept on this device and sent when the link returns (`npx snapback4 guide sync`).".to_owned(),
    49	            Offline::Tables { synced, .. } if synced.is_empty() => format!("no table in `snapback/*.q` declares `sync`, so reads need the server. Writes made offline are kept {} and sent when the link returns. Add `sync` to a table to read it offline (`npx snapback4 guide sync`).", if platform == Platform::Web { "in this browser's principal journal" } else { "on this device" }),
    50	            Offline::Tables { synced, total } if synced.len() >= *total => "every table declares `sync`, so the app reads and writes offline and syncs when the link returns.".to_owned(),
    51	            Offline::Tables { synced, .. } => format!("the synced {} ({}) {} and {} offline and {} when the link returns; the other tables read from the server, and writes to them made offline are sent when the link returns (`npx snapback4 guide sync`).",
    52	                if synced.len() == 1 { "table" } else { "tables" }, synced.iter().map(|name| format!("`{name}`")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "),
    53	                if synced.len() == 1 { "reads" } else { "read" }, if synced.len() == 1 { "writes" } else { "write" }, if synced.len() == 1 { "syncs" } else { "sync" }),
    54	        };
    55	        wrap(&format!("{lead} {body}"), 80)
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    /// init's one-line summary of the same fact, or nothing when unknown.
    59	    pub(super) fn summary(&self) -> &'static str {
    60	        match self {
    61	            Offline::Unknown => "",
    62	            Offline::Tables { synced, .. } if synced.is_empty() => ", online reads (no table declares `sync`; offline writes queue in the write journal)",
    63	            Offline::Tables { .. } => ", offline-capable for its synced tables",
    64	        }
    65	    }
    66	}
    67	
    68	/// Wrap at `width`, never inside a `code span`.
    69	fn wrap(text: &str, width: usize) -> String {
    70	    let mut words: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    71	    for word in text.split(' ') {
    72	        match words.last_mut() {
    73	            Some(open) if open.matches('`').count() % 2 == 1 => { open.push(' '); open.push_str(word); }
    74	            _ => words.push(word.to_owned()),
    75	        }
    76	    }
    77	    let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    78	    for word in &words {
    79	        match lines.last_mut() {
    80	            Some(line) if line.len() + 1 + word.len() <= width => { line.push(' '); line.push_str(word); }
    81	            _ => lines.push(word.clone()),
    82	        }
    83	    }
    84	    lines.join("\n")
    85	}
    86	
    87	/// A README with its offline paragraph replaced by `OFFLINE`, and 0.4.22's
    88	/// claim in the opening paragraph rewritten to today's opening, so `init
    89	/// --adapt` recognizes an unmodified starter README whatever its schema
    90	/// synced when it was written.
    91	pub(super) fn without_offline(text: &str) -> String {
    92	    let legacy = [
    93	        ("the Snapback 4 web starter. It works offline and syncs when the link returns.\n", "the Snapback 4 web starter.\n\nOFFLINE\n"),
    94	        ("started from the Snapback 4 Expo starter. Development and production builds\nopen the native device and work offline; Expo Go uses the online client; web\nuses the wasm device.\n",
    95	            "started from the Snapback 4 Expo starter. Development and production builds\nopen the native device; Expo Go uses the online client; web uses the wasm\ndevice.\n\nOFFLINE\n"),
    96	    ];
    97	    let mut text = text.to_owned();
    98	    for (old, new) in legacy { text = text.replacen(old, new, 1); }
    99	    text = text.replace("web uses the principal write journal (no synced tables)", "web uses the wasm\ndevice")
   100	        .replace("or the principal write journal (web, no synced tables)", "or `webDevice()` (web)")
   101	        .replace("native device and work offline;", "native device;")
   102	        .replace("web uses the wasm\ndevice", "web uses the wasm device")
   103	        .replace("web uses the\nwasm device", "web uses the wasm device");
   104	    let mut masked = text.split("\n\n").map(|paragraph| if paragraph.starts_with("Offline") { "OFFLINE" } else { paragraph }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n\n");
   105	    if masked.starts_with("# Todos") && !masked.contains("\n\nOFFLINE\n\n") {
   106	        masked = masked.replacen("wasm device.\n\n", "wasm device.\n\nOFFLINE\n\n", 1);
   107	    }
   108	    masked
   109	}
   110	
   111	/// Which starter screen pair: the guest sign-in, or the persona picker.
   112	#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
   113	pub(super) enum Platform { Web, Expo }
   114	
   115	impl Platform {
   116	    /// Where the starter writes its screen and its client.
   117	    pub(super) fn paths(self) -> (&'static str, &'static str) {
   118	        match self { Platform::Web => ("src/App.tsx", "src/client.ts"), Platform::Expo => ("App.tsx", "src/client.ts") }
   119	    }
   120	}
   121	
   122	/// The screen a signed-in viewer sees: the starter's `Todos`, which calls
   123	/// list/add/toggle/remove, or `Home`, which compiles against any schema (the
   124	/// viewer and the generated api's operation names, for the project to replace),
   125	/// with one real read when the schema has a query that takes no required
   126	/// arguments (its `api` path, `notes` or `module.notes`).
   127	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — a starter screen must typecheck against the schema beside it.
   128	// @ref LLP 3000.013#6.6 — wave 5: the adapted Home renders one useQuery through matchRead, the idioms Todos taught.
   129	#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
   130	pub(super) enum Body { Todos, Home(Option<String>) }
   131	
   132	/// The starter's screen and client for a schema: the guest sign-in, or, with
   133	/// `personas`, the persona picker, around `body`. The texts are as the
   134	/// starter ships them, before init's port rewrite.
   135	fn screens_version(platform: Platform, personas: Option<&[String]>, body: &Body, previous: bool) -> (String, String) {
   136	    let version = |current, old| if previous { old } else { current };
   137	    let (guest, guest_client, persona, persona_client, todos, home) = match platform {
   138	        Platform::Web => (include_str!("starter/web/guest.tsx"), version(include_str!("starter/web/client.ts"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/web-client.ts")), include_str!("starter/personas/web-app.tsx"), include_str!("starter/personas/web-client.ts"), version(include_str!("starter/web/todos.tsx"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/web-todos.tsx")), include_str!("starter/web/home.tsx")),
   139	        Platform::Expo => (include_str!("starter/expo/guest.tsx"), version(include_str!("starter/expo/client.ts"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/expo-client.ts")), version(include_str!("starter/personas/expo-app.tsx"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/expo-personas.tsx")), include_str!("starter/personas/expo-client.ts"), version(include_str!("starter/expo/todos.tsx"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/expo-todos.tsx")), include_str!("starter/expo/home.tsx")),
   140	    };
   141	    let first_read = match platform { Platform::Web => include_str!("starter/web/first-read.tsx"), Platform::Expo => include_str!("starter/expo/first-read.tsx") };
   142	    let (head, client) = match personas {
   143	        None => (guest, guest_client.to_owned()),
   144	        Some(personas) => {
   145	            let list = personas.iter().map(|name| serde_json::to_string(name).unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
   146	            (persona, persona_client.replace("PERSONAS", &format!("[{list}]")))
   147	        }
   148	    };
   149	    let app = match body {
   150	        Body::Todos => compose(head, todos),
   151	        Body::Home(op) => {
   152	            let (head, home) = if personas.is_some() { (persona_head_home(head), persona_home(home)) } else { (head.replace("<Todos />", "<Home />"), home.to_owned()) };
   153	            match op {
   154	                None => compose(&head, &home.replace("    <FirstRead />\n", "")),
   155	                Some(op) => compose(&compose(&head, &home), &first_read.replace("api.OP", &format!("api.{op}"))),
   156	            }
   157	        }
   158	    };
   159	    (super::without_design_references(&app), super::without_design_references(&client))
   160	}
   161	
   162	// @ref LLP 3000.013.000#17 — both identity modes choose the no-device journal when nothing syncs.
   163	pub(super) fn web_client(client: String, offline: &Offline) -> String {
   164	    if matches!(offline, Offline::Tables { synced, .. } if synced.is_empty()) {
   165	        client.replace("import { webDevice } from \"snapback4/web\";\n", "")
   166	            .replace("tier === \"web\" ? webDevice() : ", "")
   167	            .replace("device: webDevice(), ", "").replace(", device: webDevice()", "")
   168	            .replace("each with its own device and store", "each with its own principal write journal")
   169	            .replace("web uses the wasm device", "web uses the principal write journal (no synced tables)")
   170	            .replace("web uses the\nwasm device", "web uses the principal write journal (no synced tables)")
   171	    } else { client }
   172	}
   173	
   174	/// Behind the persona picker there is no session: "Sign out" (`auth.logout()`)
   175	/// showed "Signed out" and then a "Choose a persona" button, two steps for a
   176	/// switch (wave 5 lane 062). The persona Home takes the picker's `leave` and
   177	/// says "Switch persona"; the guest Home keeps its sign-out.
   178	// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.10 — the adapted persona Home switches persona; it never signs out.
   179	fn persona_head_home(head: &str) -> String {
   180	    let leave = ["() => setPersona(null)", "() => choose(null)"].into_iter().find(|leave| head.contains(&format!("<Account leave={{{leave}}}><Todos /></Account>")))
   181	        .expect("the persona heads render <Account leave={…}><Todos /></Account>");
   182	    head.replace(&format!("<Account leave={{{leave}}}><Todos /></Account>"), &format!("<Account leave={{{leave}}}><Home leave={{{leave}}} /></Account>"))
   183	}
   184	
   185	fn persona_home(home: &str) -> String {
   186	    let replacements = [
   187	        ("function Home() {", "function Home({ leave }: { leave: () => void }) {"),
   188	        ("  const auth = useAuth(), link = useLink();", "  const link = useLink();"),
   189	        (r#"<Button title="Sign out" onPress={() => void auth.logout()} />"#, r#"<Button title="Switch persona" onPress={leave} />"#),
   190	        (r#"<button onClick={() => void auth.logout()}>Sign out</button>"#, r#"<button onClick={leave}>Switch persona</button>"#),
   191	        ("// Never null here: Account renders this screen only once signed in.", "// Never null here: Account renders this screen only once the persona's app is open."),
   192	    ];
   193	    let mut home = home.to_owned();
   194	    for (from, to) in replacements { home = home.replace(from, to); }
   195	    assert!(!home.contains("auth.") && home.contains("Switch persona"), "the persona Home has no session to sign out of");
   196	    home.replace("import { useAuth, useLink, useSignedInViewer }", "import { useLink, useSignedInViewer }")
   197	}
   198	
   199	/// One module from a sign-in head and a screen body, each written as a whole
   200	/// file: their single-line named imports merge per module (packages before
   201	/// `../`, then `./`), then the head's code, then the body's.
   202	fn compose(head: &str, body: &str) -> String {
   203	    fn split(text: &str) -> (Vec<&str>, &str) {
   204	        let mut imports = Vec::new();
   205	        let mut rest = text;
   206	        while let Some(line) = rest.split_inclusive('\n').next().filter(|line| line.starts_with("import ")) {
   207	            imports.push(line.trim_end());
   208	            rest = &rest[line.len()..];
   209	        }
   210	        (imports, rest.trim_start_matches('\n'))
   211	    }
   212	    let (head_imports, head_code) = split(head);
   213	    let (body_imports, body_code) = split(body);
   214	    // (`import {` or `import type {`, module, names) in first-seen order.
   215	    let mut merged: Vec<(String, String, Vec<String>)> = Vec::new();
   216	    for line in head_imports.into_iter().chain(body_imports) {
   217	        let (Some(open), Some(close)) = (line.find('{'), line.find('}')) else { panic!("starter imports are `import {{ … }} from \"…\";`: {line}") };
   218	        let (kind, module) = (line[..open].trim().to_owned(), line[close + 1..].trim().to_owned());
   219	        let names = line[open + 1..close].split(',').map(|name| name.trim().to_owned()).filter(|name| !name.is_empty());
   220	        match merged.iter_mut().find(|(k, m, _)| *k == kind && *m == module) {
   221	            Some((_, _, existing)) => for name in names { if !existing.contains(&name) { existing.push(name); } },
   222	            None => merged.push((kind, module, names.collect())),
   223	        }
   224	    }
   225	    merged.sort_by_key(|(_, module, _)| if module.contains("\"../") { 1 } else if module.contains("\"./") { 2 } else { 0 });
   226	    let imports = merged.iter().map(|(kind, module, names)| format!("{kind} {{ {} }} {module}\n", names.join(", "))).collect::<String>();
   227	    format!("{imports}\n{head_code}\n{body_code}")
   228	}
   229	
   230	const GUEST_SIGN_IN: &str = "Choose \"Continue as a guest\" to sign in (`use identity`); `npx snapback4 guide personas`\nadds password accounts and development personas.";
   231	const PERSONA_SIGN_IN: &str = "The first screen (on the web also `?as=<name>`) acts as a persona your schema declares with\n`use personas`: `usePersonaApp` opens one app per persona and closes the old one on a switch.\nPersonas act only on `snapback4 dev`: add `use identity` and a sign-in screen before you\ndeploy (`npx snapback4 guide personas`).";
   232	
   233	/// The starter README for a screen: the Todos README describes the starter's
   234	/// own schema and screen; beside a project's schema (the neutral `Home`) the
   235	/// README is named after the directory and describes that schema's sign-in.
   236	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (042): an adapted starter kept a README about todos and guest sign-in.
   237	pub(super) fn readme(platform: Platform, title: &str, body: &Body, personas: bool, offline: &Offline) -> String {
   238	    let todos = match platform { Platform::Web => include_str!("starter/web/README.md"), Platform::Expo => include_str!("starter/expo/README.md") };
   239	    if *body == Body::Todos {
   240	        return todos.replace("native device and work offline;", "native device;")
   241	            .replace("wasm device.\n", &format!("wasm device.\n\n{}\n", offline.paragraph(platform)));
   242	    }
   243	    let home = match platform { Platform::Web => include_str!("starter/web/README.home.md"), Platform::Expo => include_str!("starter/expo/README.home.md") };
   244	    let text = home.replacen("# TITLE", &format!("# {title}"), 1).replace("SIGN_IN", if personas { PERSONA_SIGN_IN } else { GUEST_SIGN_IN })
   245	        .replacen("OFFLINE\n", &format!("{}\n", offline.paragraph(platform)), 1);
   246	    if platform == Platform::Web { return text; }
   247	    // The Expo toolchain notes are the Todos README's own, word for word.
   248	    let advisories = todos.split_once("\n> ").map(|(_, rest)| format!("> {}", rest.split_once("\n\n").expect("the advisory box ends").0)).expect("the Expo README has its advisory box");
   249	    let toolchain = todos.split_once("\nKeep the Expo and").map(|(_, rest)| format!("Keep the Expo and{}", rest.split_once("## Next steps").expect("the Expo README has next steps").0)).expect("the Expo README has its toolchain notes");
   250	    text.replacen("ADVISORIES", &advisories, 1).replacen("TOOLCHAIN\n", &toolchain, 1)
   251	}
   252	
   253	/// The Expo app's `app.json` ids, named after the directory's title: a slug
   254	/// (`bake-sale`), a URL scheme and a bundle id that start with a letter
   255	/// (`bakesale`, `dev.snapback4.bakesale`).
   256	pub(super) fn app_ids(title: &str) -> (String, String, String) {
   257	    let words: Vec<String> = title.split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_alphanumeric()).filter(|word| !word.is_empty()).map(str::to_ascii_lowercase).collect();
   258	    let slug = if words.is_empty() { "app".to_owned() } else { words.join("-") };
   259	    let mut scheme = words.concat();
   260	    if !scheme.starts_with(|c: char| c.is_ascii_alphabetic()) { scheme = format!("app{scheme}"); }
   261	    let bundle = format!("dev.snapback4.{scheme}");
   262	    (slug, scheme, bundle)
   263	}
   264	
   265	/// What 0.4.21 and earlier wrote into every Expo starter's `app.json`.
   266	pub(super) const TODO_IDS: [(&str, &str); 3] = [("slug", "snapback4-todos"), ("scheme", "snapback4todos"), ("bundle", "dev.snapback4.todos")];
   267	
   268	pub(super) fn web_files(shape: &Shape) -> Vec<(&'static str, String)> {
   269	    let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../../../../apps/chat/package.json")).unwrap();
   270	    package["name"] = json!("snapback4-web-starter");
   271	    package["version"] = json!("0.1.0");
   272	    package["dependencies"]["snapback4"] = json!(env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
   273	    package["scripts"]["build"] = json!("vite build && snapback4 web-shell dist");
   274	    package["scripts"]["build:web"] = package["scripts"]["build"].clone();
   275	    let (app, client) = identity_screens(Platform::Web, shape.personas.as_deref(), &shape.body, shape.password);
   276	    let client = web_client(client, &shape.offline);
   277	    vec![
   278	        ("package.json", super::pretty(&package)),
   279	        ("index.html", include_str!("starter/web/index.html").replace("TITLE", &html_text(&shape.title))),
   280	        ("vite.config.mts", include_str!("starter/web/vite.config.mts").to_owned()),
   281	        ("tsconfig.json", super::esm_vite_config(include_str!("../../../../../apps/chat/tsconfig.json"))),
   282	        ("public/favicon.svg", include_str!("starter/favicon.svg").to_owned()),
   283	        ("src/main.tsx", include_str!("starter/web/main.tsx").to_owned()),
   284	        ("src/styles.css", include_str!("starter/web/styles.css").to_owned()),
   285	        ("src/App.tsx", app),
   286	        ("src/client.ts", client),
   287	        ("src/vite-env.d.ts", include_str!("../../../../../apps/chat/src/vite-env.d.ts").to_owned()),
   288	        ("snapback/schema.q", SCHEMA.to_owned()),
   289	        ("README.md", identity_readme(Platform::Web, &shape.title, &shape.body, shape.personas.is_some(), &shape.offline, shape.password)),
   290	    ].into_iter().chain(FIXTURES.map(|(name, text)| (name, text.to_owned()))).collect()
     1	//! Starter files are embedded at build time; the packaged binary needs no checkout.
     2	
     3	use super::Options;
     4	use crate::project::{GENERATED_PACKAGE, GITIGNORE};
     5	use serde_json::{Value, json};
     6	use std::{io::Write, path::Path};
     7	
     8	mod web;
     9	mod social;
    10	mod starter;
    11	mod adapt;
    12	mod ports;
    13	pub(crate) use adapt::{report as starter_leftovers, report_json as starter_leftovers_json};
    14	
    15	const USAGE: &str = "usage: snapback4 init [dir] [--online|--expo|--web]";
    16	
    17	pub fn init(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    18	    // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.2 — the init-first path: fit the starter already written to the schema written since.
    19	    if options.flags.contains_key("adapt") {
    20	        if options.positionals.len() > 1 || options.flags.keys().any(|flag| !matches!(flag.as_str(), "adapt" | "web" | "expo")) {
    21	            eprintln!("usage: snapback4 init [dir] --adapt\n  --adapt fits the starter files init wrote to your snapback/*.q; it takes no other starter flags");
    22	            return 2;
    23	        }
    24	        return adapt::adapt(options.positionals.first().map(Path::new).unwrap_or_else(|| Path::new(".")));
    25	    }
    26	    let template = options.flags.get("template").map(String::as_str);
    27	    let modes = ["online", "expo", "web"].iter().filter(|flag| options.flags.contains_key(**flag)).count();
    28	    // `--template social` is a whole app; `--template chat` is the reference
    29	    // chat app for one client, `--web` or `--expo`.
    30	    let template_ok = match template {
    31	        None => true,
    32	        Some("social") => modes == 0,
    33	        Some("chat") => modes == 1 && !options.flags.contains_key("online"),
    34	        Some(_) => false,
    35	    };
    36	    if !template_ok || (template.is_some() && options.flags.contains_key("recipe")) {
    37	        eprintln!("usage: snapback4 new <dir> --template social\n       snapback4 init [dir] --web --template chat\n       snapback4 init [dir] --expo --template chat");
    38	        return 2;
    39	    }
    40	    let social = template == Some("social");
    41	    let chat = template == Some("chat");
    42	    // @ref LLP 3000.007.003#7 — new apps declare timelines; installed recipes retain their library support.
    43	    let retired = ["prefix", "authors", "edges", "public-profiles", "public-posts", "public-follows", "public-reposts"];
    44	    if options.flags.get("recipe").is_some_and(|name| name == "feeds")
    45	        || retired.iter().any(|flag| options.flags.contains_key(*flag)) {
    46	        eprintln!("E_RECIPE_RETIRED: the Following recipe installer is retired; no files written\n  rewrite: maintain feed: timeline\n    events posts by authorId, at removed by removed\n    reposts reposts by actorId, postId, at\n    edges follows by followerId, followedId\n  use a fresh feed name; dev adopts retained sources and deploy requires --allow-feed-adoption; see snapback4 guide feeds");
    47	        return 2;
    48	    }
    49	    if options.positionals.len() > 1 || modes > 1 || options.flags.contains_key("recipe") {
    50	        eprintln!("{USAGE}");
    51	        return 2;
    52	    }
    53	    let range = match ports::range(options) {
    54	        Ok(range) => range,
    55	        Err(error) => { eprintln!("E_INPUT: {error}"); return 2; }
    56	    };
    57	    let port = match options.flags.get("port").map(|port| port.parse::<u16>()) {
    58	        None => range.map(|(low, _)| low),
    59	        Some(Ok(port)) if port > 0 => Some(port),
    60	        Some(_) => {
    61	            eprintln!("E_INPUT at init --port: expected a port number from 1 to 65535\n  rewrite: snapback4 init --web --port 4401");
    62	            return 2;
    63	        }
    64	    };
    65	    let skip_existing = options.flags.contains_key("skip-existing");
    66	    let dir = options.positionals.first().map(Path::new).unwrap_or_else(|| Path::new("."));
    67	    let online = options.flags.contains_key("online");
    68	    let expo = social || options.flags.contains_key("expo");
    69	    let web = options.flags.contains_key("web");
    70	    // The Vite page's own port: explicit, or the one after the backend's so a
    71	    // project confined to a port range stays inside it.
    72	    let web_port = match options.flags.get("web-port").map(|port| port.parse::<u16>()) {
    73	        _ if options.flags.contains_key("web-port") && !web => {
    74	            eprintln!("E_INPUT at init --web-port: only the Vite starters (--web) serve a page of their own; Expo's web runs on `npx expo start --port N`\n  rewrite: snapback4 init --web --port 4401 --web-port 4402");
    75	            return 2;
    76	        }
    77	        Some(Ok(port)) if port > 0 => Some(port),

     2	//! is built, `.snapback4/` is the data directory. Nothing here is committed
     3	//! except the `.q` files.

     5	use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError, Store};

     8	
     9	pub struct Project {
    10	    pub root: PathBuf,
    11	}
    12	
    13	#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
    14	pub struct SourceStamp { sources: u128, dependencies: u128 }
    15	type ServerDependency = (String, Option<(String, String)>);
    16	
    17	fn stamp(value: impl std::fmt::Debug) -> u128 {
    18	    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    19	    let hash = Sha256::digest(format!("{value:?}"));
    20	    u128::from_be_bytes(hash[..16].try_into().expect("sixteen hash bytes"))
    21	}
    22	
    23	impl Project {
    24	    /// Walk up from `start` to the directory holding any `snapback/*.q`.
    25	    // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.1 — any Quarry module is the project; no invented root schema is needed.
    26	    pub fn locate(start: &Path) -> Option<Self> {
    27	        let mut dir = Some(start.to_path_buf());
    28	        while let Some(current) = dir {
    29	            if std::fs::read_dir(current.join("snapback")).ok().is_some_and(|entries| entries.flatten().any(|entry| entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().ends_with(".q") && entry.path().is_file())) {
    30	                return Some(Self { root: current });
    31	            }
    32	            dir = current.parent().map(Path::to_path_buf);
    33	        }
    34	        None
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    pub fn schema_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
    38	        self.root.join("snapback")
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    pub fn server_source(&self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    42	        ["snapback/server.ts", "snapback/server.mts", "snapback/server.mjs"].into_iter().map(|p| self.root.join(p)).find(|p| p.is_file())
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    pub fn generated_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
    46	        self.schema_dir().join("generated")
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    /// The app's native crate (LLP 3000.005), when it has one.
    50	    pub fn native_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
    51	        self.schema_dir().join("native")
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    pub fn native_manifest(&self) -> PathBuf {
    55	        self.native_dir().join("Cargo.toml")
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    /// The built module, beside the generated types.
    59	    pub fn native_module(&self) -> PathBuf {
    60	        self.generated_dir().join("native.wasm")
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    /// The schema's Rust types for the native crate to include.
    64	    pub fn native_types(&self) -> PathBuf {
    65	        self.generated_dir().join("native.rs")
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    pub fn data_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
    69	        self.root.join(".snapback4")
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    pub fn store_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
    73	        self.data_dir().join("store.sqlite")
    74	    }
    75	
    76	    /// Every `.q` file under `snapback/`, `schema.q` first, as `(name, text)`.
    77	    pub fn sources(&self) -> std::io::Result<Vec<(String, String)>> {
    78	        let mut names: Vec<String> = std::fs::read_dir(self.schema_dir())?
    79	            .flatten()
    80	            .filter_map(|entry| {
    81	                let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string();
    82	                (name.ends_with(".q") && entry.path().is_file()).then_some(name)
    83	            })
    84	            .collect();
    85	        names.sort();
    86	        if let Some(position) = names.iter().position(|n| n == "schema.q") {
    87	            let schema = names.remove(position);
    88	            names.insert(0, schema);
    89	        }
    90	        names.into_iter().map(|name| Ok((name.clone(), std::fs::read_to_string(self.schema_dir().join(&name))?))).collect()
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    /// The newest modification time among the sources — the `.q` files and
    94	    /// the native crate's manifest and `src/` — for the watcher.
    95	    pub fn sources_stamp(&self) -> SourceStamp {
    96	        fn newest(dir: &Path, keep: &dyn Fn(&Path) -> bool, depth: u8) -> u128 {
    97	            std::fs::read_dir(dir)
    98	                .map(|entries| {
    99	                    entries
   100	                        .flatten()
   101	                        .map(|e| e.path())
   102	                        .map(|path| {
   103	                            if path.is_dir() && depth > 0 {
   104	                                newest(&path, keep, depth - 1)
   105	                            } else if keep(&path) {
   106	                                path.metadata().ok().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()).and_then(|t| t.duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).ok()).map(|d| d.as_nanos()).unwrap_or(0)
   107	                            } else {
   108	                                0
   109	                            }
   110	                        })
   111	                        .max()
   112	                        .unwrap_or(0)
   113	                })
   114	                .unwrap_or(0)
   115	        }
   116	        let quarry = newest(&self.schema_dir(), &|path| path.extension().is_some_and(|x| x == "q"), 0);
   117	        let native = newest(&self.native_dir(), &|path| path.extension().is_some_and(|x| x == "rs" || x == "toml"), 6);
   118	        // Include server source names as well as mtimes, so deletion of a
   119	        // non-newest imported module still triggers validation and restart.
   120	        fn server_files(dir: &Path, files: &mut Vec<(String, u128, u64)>, depth: u8) {
   121	            if depth == 0 { return; }
   122	            if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir) {
   123	                for entry in entries.flatten() {
   124	                    let path = entry.path();
   125	                    let Ok(meta) = entry.metadata() else { continue; };
   126	                    if entry.file_type().is_ok_and(|kind| kind.is_symlink()) { continue; }
   127	                    if meta.is_dir() {
   128	                        if !matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(), Some("generated" | "native" | "node_modules" | "target")) { server_files(&path, files, depth - 1); }
   129	                    } else if path.extension().is_some_and(|ext| matches!(ext.to_str(), Some("ts" | "mts" | "mjs" | "js" | "json"))) {
   130	                        files.push((path.to_string_lossy().into(), meta.modified().ok().and_then(|at| at.duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).ok()).map(|at| at.as_nanos()).unwrap_or(0), meta.len()));
   131	                    }
   132	                }
   133	            }
   134	        }
   135	        let mut files = Vec::new(); server_files(&self.schema_dir(), &mut files, 12); files.sort();
   136	        // The TypeScript checker records imports/configs outside snapback/ as
   137	        // well as failed resolution candidates. Stat that bounded graph, not
   138	        // unrelated UI sources, and retain absence so deletion/refill wakes us.
   139	        let dependencies: Vec<ServerDependency> = self.checked_dependencies().into_iter().map(|(path, _)| {
   140	            let state = std::fs::metadata(&path).ok().map(|meta| (
   141	                meta.modified().ok().and_then(|at| at.duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).ok()).map(|at| at.as_nanos()).unwrap_or(0).to_string(),
   142	                meta.len().to_string(),
   143	            ));
   144	            (path, state)
   145	        }).collect();

     3	import { readFileSync, existsSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
     4	import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
     5	import { createRequire } from 'node:module';

     7	
     8	function run() {
     9	  const args = process.argv.slice(2);

    11	  if (args.length > 1 || (args[0] && !/^(latest|\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?)$/.test(args[0]))) throw Error('E_INPUT: upgrade takes an exact version or latest');
    12	  let root = process.cwd();
    13	  while (!existsSync(join(root, 'package.json'))) { const parent = dirname(root); if (parent === root) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: no project package.json'); root = parent; }
    14	  const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
    15	  const schema = existsSync(join(root, 'snapback')) && readdirSync(join(root, 'snapback')).some(file => file.endsWith('.q'));
    16	  const cli = () => {

    19	  };
    20	  const check = () => spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli(), 'check', '--json'], { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8', stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
    21	  if (schema) {
    22	    // A previous package may predate snapshots. Capture its own compiler output
    23	    // before install; never infer old facts using the newly installed engine.
    24	    const old = check();
    25	    let facts;
    26	    try { facts = JSON.parse(old.stdout); } catch { /* Unavailable baseline remains explicit. */ }
    27	    const api = join(root, 'snapback/generated/api.ts');
    28	    if (facts && existsSync(api) && old.status === 0) {
    29	      const counts = values => (values ?? []).reduce((out, value) => { out[value.code] = (out[value.code] ?? 0) + 1; return out; }, {});
    30	      const require = createRequire(join(root, 'package.json'));
    31	      const version = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dirname(dirname(require.resolve('snapback4/bin-path'))), 'package.json'), 'utf8')).version;
    32	      const sources = readdirSync(join(root, 'snapback')).filter(file => file.endsWith('.q')).sort((a, b) => a === 'schema.q' ? -1 : b === 'schema.q' ? 1 : a.localeCompare(b)).map(file => [file, readFileSync(join(root, 'snapback', file), 'utf8')]);
    33	      mkdirSync(join(root, '.snapback4'), { recursive: true });
    34	      writeFileSync(join(root, '.snapback4/check-surface.json'), JSON.stringify({ version, sources, api: readFileSync(api, 'utf8'), notes: counts(facts.notes), refusals: counts(facts.problems) }));
    35	    } else console.log('upgrade: schema comparison baseline unavailable; old check did not complete');
    36	  }
    37	  const declared = pkg.packageManager?.split('@')[0];
    38	  const locks = [['bun', ['bun.lock', 'bun.lockb']], ['pnpm', ['pnpm-lock.yaml']], ['npm', ['package-lock.json', 'npm-shrinkwrap.json']]].filter(([, files]) => files.some(file => existsSync(join(root, file)))).map(([pm]) => pm);
    39	  if (declared && !['npm', 'bun', 'pnpm'].includes(declared)) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: supported package managers are npm, bun and pnpm');
    40	  if (!declared && locks.length > 1) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: multiple package-manager lockfiles; set packageManager in package.json');
    41	  const pm = declared ?? locks[0] ?? 'npm';
    42	  const dependencies = ['dependencies', 'devDependencies', 'optionalDependencies'];
    43	  const targets = ['snapback4', 'snapback4-expo'].filter(name => name === 'snapback4' || dependencies.some(key => pkg[key]?.[name]) || existsSync(join(root, 'node_modules', name, 'package.json')));
    44	  const invoke = (command, capture = false) => {
    45	    const result = spawnSync(pm, command, { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8', stdio: capture ? ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] : 'inherit' });
    46	    if (result.error || result.status !== 0) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: ${pm} ${command[0]} failed; check registry/tool availability`);
    47	    return result.stdout;
    48	  };
    49	  let version = args[0] ?? 'latest';
    50	  if (version === 'latest') {
    51	    const text = invoke(pm === 'bun' ? ['info', 'snapback4@latest', 'version', '--json'] : ['view', 'snapback4@latest', 'version', '--json'], true).trim();
    52	    try { const data = JSON.parse(text); version = typeof data === 'string' ? data : data.version; }
    53	    catch { throw Error('E_UPGRADE: package manager returned no exact core version'); }
    54	  }
    55	  if (typeof version !== 'string' || !/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$/.test(version)) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: registry returned no exact core version');

    57	  for (const name of targets) invoke(pm === 'bun' ? ['info', `${name}@${version}`, 'version', '--json'] : ['view', `${name}@${version}`, 'version', '--json'], true);
    58	  // Preserve each dependency section. One resolved version, even across sections.
    59	  const groups = new Map();
    60	  for (const name of targets) {
    61	    const sections = dependencies.filter(key => pkg[key]?.[name]);
    62	    if (sections.length > 1) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: ${name} occurs in multiple dependency sections; consolidate it first`);
    63	    const section = sections[0] ?? 'dependencies';
    64	    if (!groups.has(section)) groups.set(section, []);
    65	    groups.get(section).push(`${name}@${version}`);
    66	  }
    67	  for (const [section, packages] of groups) {
    68	    const mode = section === 'devDependencies' ? (pm === 'bun' ? '--dev' : '--save-dev') : section === 'optionalDependencies' ? (pm === 'bun' ? '--optional' : '--save-optional') : null;
    69	    invoke([pm === 'npm' ? 'install' : 'add', pm === 'bun' ? '--exact' : '--save-exact', ...(mode ? [mode] : []), ...packages]);
    70	  }
    71	  const after = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
    72	  for (const name of targets) if (!dependencies.some(key => after[key]?.[name] === version)) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: ${name} was not pinned to ${version}`);
    73	  const exact = existsSync(join(root, 'exact.mjs')) || existsSync(join(root, 'exact.json')) || dependencies.some(key => Object.keys(pkg[key] ?? {}).some(name => /^exact2?(?:$|[-/])/.test(name)));
    74	  if (exact) console.log('Exact device rebuild: bun exact.mjs snapback --rebuild'); // HS owns this command and the pairing gate.
    75	  if (schema) {
    76	    const checked = check();
    77	    if (checked.stderr) process.stderr.write(checked.stderr);
    78	    if (checked.stdout) process.stdout.write(checked.stdout);
    79	    if (checked.error || checked.status !== 0) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: packages pinned to ${version}; post-upgrade check refused. Repair the reported schema/device issues and run check again.`);
    80	  }
    81	  console.log(`upgrade: ${targets.join(', ')} pinned to ${version} with ${pm}; commit the lockfile, run check and acceptance tests${targets.includes('snapback4-expo') ? ', and rebuild the native app' : ''}`);
    82	}
    83	try { run(); } catch (error) { console.error(error.message); process.exitCode = 1; }
     1	import { useState } from "react";

     8	function Todos() {
     9	  const auth = useAuth(), link = useLink();

    11	  const viewer = useSignedInViewer();

    13	  // unconfirmed writes (marked `pending`) and changes from other devices.
    14	  // usePage is for a query that takes `c: cursor ?`; any other query reads

    19	  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
    20	  const submit = () => {
    21	    if (!title.trim()) return;
    22	    void add.run({ title: title.trim() });

    24	  };
    25	  const failed = [add.last, toggle.last, remove.last].find(isFailed);
    26	  return <View style={{ flex: 1, padding: 24, gap: 12 }}>
    27	    <Text style={{ fontSize: 24 }}>Todos</Text>
    28	    <Text>{viewer} · {tier} · {link === "online" ? "online" : "offline"}</Text>
    29	    <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", gap: 8 }}>
    30	      <TextInput accessibilityLabel="Task" style={{ flex: 1, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: "#ccc", padding: 8 }} value={title} onChangeText={setTitle}
    31	        placeholder="What needs doing?" maxLength={200} onSubmitEditing={submit} />
    32	      <Button title="Add" onPress={submit} />
    33	    </View>
    34	    <Text accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">{add.last?.state ?? "Ready"}</Text>
    35	    {failed && <Text style={{ color: "crimson" }}>{failed.why.message}</Text>}
    36	    <TodoList todos={todos} toggle={toggle} remove={remove} />
    37	    <Button title="Sign out" onPress={() => void auth.logout()} />
    38	  </View>;
    39	}
    40	
    41	// Hook results are named by the operation for props: UsePage<typeof api.list>,
    42	// UseMutation<typeof api.toggle>, and UseQuery<typeof api.op> for useQuery.
    43	function TodoList({ todos, toggle, remove }: { todos: UsePage<typeof api.list>; toggle: UseMutation<typeof api.toggle>; remove: UseMutation<typeof api.remove> }) {
    44	  return <>
    45	    {isLoading(todos.read) && <Text>Loading…</Text>}
    46	    {isDenied(todos.read) && <Text style={{ color: "crimson" }}>{todos.read.denied.message}</Text>}
    47	    {todos.items.map(todo => <View key={todo.id} style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, opacity: todo.pending ? 0.6 : 1 }}>
    48	      <Pressable style={{ flex: 1 }} onPress={() => void toggle.run({ todoId: todo.id })}>
    49	        <Text style={{ textDecorationLine: todo.done ? "line-through" : "none" }}>{todo.done ? "☑" : "☐"} {todo.title}</Text>
    50	      </Pressable>
    51	      <Button title="Delete" onPress={() => void remove.run({ todoId: todo.id })} />
    52	    </View>)}
    53	    {todos.hasMore && <Button title="Show more" onPress={todos.loadMore} />}
    54	  </>;
    55	}

# Todos: a Snapback 4 Expo starter

An Expo (React Native) app over a local-first Snapback 4 backend: one table,
one live query and three mutations. Development and production builds open the
native device and work offline; Expo Go uses the online client; web uses the
wasm device.

> **`npm install` takes a few minutes and reports about 15 high-severity
> advisories. They are expected; do not run `npm audit fix --force`** (it
> "fixes" them by downgrading Expo to SDK 44). All come from two packages in
> Expo's development toolchain with no patched release; neither is in the app
> bundle. Details under [Audit findings](#audit-findings).

```sh
npm install
npx snapback4 check      # compiles snapback/schema.q, writes snapback/generated/api.ts
npx snapback4 dev        # serves http://127.0.0.1:4400 and reloads on save
npm start                # in a second terminal: Expo Go, or press w for web
npm run typecheck
```

`npm run ios:dev` builds the native development build. The iOS simulator
reaches http://127.0.0.1:4400 on this machine (`init --expo --port N` writes
that port; `EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL` overrides it); for a physical phone set
`EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL` to your TLS deployment. Choose "Continue as a
guest", then add a few todos.

## Files

- `snapback/schema.q`: the backend (tables, rules, `sync`, queries, mutations).
  Edit it and `snapback4 dev` re-adopts it; `snapback4 check` regenerates the API.
- `fixtures/`: seed rows and expected outcomes for `snapback4 test`.
- `src/client.ts`: one `createApp` for the whole app; it picks the native
  device, the online client (Expo Go) or `webDevice()` (web).
- `App.tsx`: sign-in, the live list (`usePage`) and writes (`useMutation`).
  `usePage` passes the query's cursor itself: a paged query takes `c: cursor ?`
  and reads `after c` (`npx snapback4 guide reads`); any other query reads
  with `useQuery`. Props name hook results by operation (`UsePage<typeof
  api.list>`, `UseQuery<…>`, `UseMutation<…>`), and `useSignedInViewer()` is
  the viewer below the sign-in. After you replace the schema,
  `npx snapback4 init --adapt` swaps this screen for a `Home` that compiles
  against any schema, while it is unmodified.
- `expo-env.d.ts`: Expo's types, including `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_*`.
- `metro.config.js` and `metro-wasm-transformer.cjs`: keep both; Metro then
  ships the web device's wasm and skips the local `.snapback4/` store.

Keep the Expo and `babel-preset-expo` major versions together; mixing them can
fail Hermes compilation.

## Audit findings

`package.json`'s `overrides` moves the config plugins' `xcode` from the
deprecated `uuid@7` to `uuid@11` (GHSA-w5hq-g745-h8pq). `npm audit` still
reports about 15 high findings from two advisories in Expo's development
toolchain, with no patched release of either package in any version:
`braces` (GHSA-vfj7-8cjw-p6xm, a stack-exhaustion denial of service on deeply
nested glob patterns; Metro's file watcher through `micromatch`) and
`node-forge` (GHSA-86w9-cpqp-85rv, RSA signature verification; the Expo CLI's
code-signing for updates). Neither is part of the app bundle, and the starter
passes neither untrusted patterns nor signatures to them. Do not run
`npm audit fix --force`: it "fixes" them by downgrading Expo to SDK 44.
`npx expo install --fix` takes Expo 57 patch releases as they ship.

## Native proof

`npm run export:ios` publishes Hermes to `dist/` and returns at every milestone;
native proof then runs in the background at background QoS, so you can keep
building. The next export and `npx snapback4 doctor` print its result; for a
final report run `npx snapback4 witness status --wait`. Receipts and logs stay
in `artifacts/native-smoke/<timestamp>`. `npm run native:smoke` explicitly
waits for a full Release build and smoke. Smoke proves the store opens,
acquisition, cold offline reopen and reconnect; pending write replay needs an
app scenario (`npx snapback4 guide witness`).

## Next steps

- Grow the schema: `npx snapback4 guide language`, then `guide rules`, `guide sync`.
- Try operations from the terminal: `npx snapback4 run add '{"title":"milk"}' --as alice`.
- Test without a server: `npx snapback4 test --fixture fixtures --as alice` loads
  `fixtures/todos.jsonl` and runs the expectations in `fixtures/test.cases.jsonl`
  (`npx snapback4 guide testing`).
- Add email accounts: change `use identity` to `use identity password` and see `guide auth`.
- A complete multi-screen example: `npx snapback4 init <dir> --expo --template chat`.
# Todos: a Snapback 4 web starter

A Vite + React app over a local-first Snapback 4 backend: one table, one live
query and three mutations. It works offline and syncs when the link returns.

```sh
npm install
npx snapback4 check      # compiles snapback/schema.q, writes snapback/generated/api.ts
npx snapback4 dev        # serves http://127.0.0.1:4400 and reloads on save
npm run dev              # in a second terminal: http://localhost:5173
```

`snapback4 init --web --port 4401` points the client at that backend and serves
the page on the next port (4402); `--web-port N` picks the page's port, which
`vite.config.mts` holds for both `npm run dev` and `vite preview`.

Choose "Continue as a guest", then add a few todos. Open a second tab to see
live updates; stop the backend to see writes queue offline and settle when it
returns.

## Files

- `snapback/schema.q`: the backend (tables, rules, `sync`, queries, mutations).
  Edit it and `snapback4 dev` re-adopts it; `snapback4 check` regenerates the API.
- `fixtures/`: seed rows and expected outcomes for `snapback4 test`.
- `src/client.ts`: one `createApp` for the whole app. This synced todo schema
  uses `device: webDevice()`; a bare `createClient()` in a browser refuses
  mutations with `E_ADMISSION`.
- `src/App.tsx`: sign-in, the live list (`usePage`) and writes (`useMutation`).
  `usePage` passes the query's cursor itself: a paged query takes `c: cursor ?`
  and reads `after c` (`npx snapback4 guide reads`); any other query reads
  with `useQuery`. Props name hook results by operation (`UsePage<typeof
  api.list>`, `UseQuery<…>`, `UseMutation<…>`), and `useSignedInViewer()` is
  the viewer below the sign-in. After you replace the schema,
  `npx snapback4 init --adapt` swaps this screen for a `Home` that compiles
  against any schema, while it is unmodified.
- `src/styles.css`, `index.html` (titled after this directory) and
  `vite.config.mts`: yours to change; `.mts` keeps Vite's config ESM even when
  package.json says `"type": "commonjs"`.

## Next steps

- Grow the schema: `npx snapback4 guide language`, then `guide rules`, `guide sync`.
- Try operations from the terminal: `npx snapback4 run add '{"title":"milk"}' --as alice`.
- Test without a server: `npx snapback4 test --fixture fixtures --as alice` loads
  `fixtures/todos.jsonl` and runs the expectations in `fixtures/test.cases.jsonl`
  (`npx snapback4 guide testing`).
- Add email accounts: change `use identity` to `use identity password` and see `guide auth`.
- Ship: `VITE_SNAPBACK_URL=https://your-backend npm run build` builds `dist/`
  with an offline app shell (`snapback4 web-shell dist`); serve it over HTTPS.
- A complete multi-screen example: `npx snapback4 init <dir> --web --template chat`.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#9 — one range localizes every starter launcher before any file is written.
use super::super::Options;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

pub(super) fn range(options: &Options) -> Result<Option<(u16, u16)>, String> {
    let text = match options.flags.get("port-range") {
        Some(text) => Some(text.clone()),
        None => match (std::env::var("PORT_LO").ok(), std::env::var("PORT_HI").ok()) {
            (None, None) => None,
            (Some(low), Some(high)) => Some(format!("{low}-{high}")),
            _ => return Err("PORT_LO and PORT_HI must be set together".into()),
        }
    };
    let Some(text) = text else { return Ok(None) };
    let (low, high) = text.split_once('-').ok_or("--port-range must be A-B (1 <= A <= B <= 65535)")?;
    let (low, high) = (low.parse::<u16>(), high.parse::<u16>());
    match (low, high) {
        (Ok(low), Ok(high)) if low > 0 && low <= high => Ok(Some((low, high))),
        _ => Err("--port-range must be A-B (1 <= A <= B <= 65535)".into()),
    }
}

pub(super) fn scripts(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str, String)>, backend: u16, frontend: u16, range: Option<(u16, u16)>, web: bool) {
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut() {
        if *name != "package.json" { continue; }
        let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).unwrap();
        package["scripts"]["backend"] = json!(format!("snapback4 dev --port {backend}"));
        if web {
            package["scripts"]["dev"] = json!(format!("vite --port {frontend} --strictPort"));
            package["scripts"]["preview"] = json!(format!("vite preview --port {frontend} --strictPort"));
        } else {
            package["scripts"]["start"] = json!(format!("expo start --port {frontend}"));
            package["scripts"]["web"] = json!(format!("expo start --web --port {frontend}"));
            package["scripts"]["native:smoke"] = json!(format!("snapback4 witness ios --build . --smoke --port {backend}"));
        }
        package["scripts"]["proof:web"] = json!(format!("snapback4 witness web dist{}", range.map(|(low, high)| format!(" --port-range {low}-{high}")).unwrap_or_default()));
        *text = super::pretty(&package);
    }
}

    if (flag === '--help') return null;
    if (!flag.startsWith('--') && i === 0) { out.export = flag; continue; }
    const key = flag.slice(2);
    if (!flags.has(key) || !args[i + 1] || args[i + 1].startsWith('--')) throw Error(`E_INPUT: witness web takes --${[...flags].join(', --')}`);
    out[key] = args[++i];
  }
  out.timeout = Number(out.timeout);
  if (!Number.isFinite(out.timeout) || out.timeout <= 0 || out.timeout > 600) throw Error('E_INPUT: --timeout must be seconds from 1 to 600');
  if (typeof out.args === 'string') out.args = JSON.parse(out.args);
  if (out.args !== undefined && (!out.args || Array.isArray(out.args) || typeof out.args !== 'object')) throw Error('E_INPUT: --args must be a JSON object');
  if (out.as && out.email) throw Error('E_INPUT: choose --as or --email');
  if (out.email && (!out['password-env'] || !process.env[out['password-env']])) throw Error('E_INPUT: --email requires --password-env naming a nonempty environment variable');
  if ([out.field, out.value, out.button].some(value => value !== undefined) && ![out.field, out.value, out.button].every(value => value !== undefined)) throw Error('E_INPUT: visible form proof requires --field LABEL --value TEXT --button LABEL');
  const range = out['port-range'] ?? (process.env.PORT_LO !== undefined || process.env.PORT_HI !== undefined ? `${process.env.PORT_LO}-${process.env.PORT_HI}` : undefined);
  if (range !== undefined) {
    const ports = /^(\d+)-(\d+)$/.exec(range);
    if (!ports || Number(ports[1]) < 1 || Number(ports[2]) > 65535 || Number(ports[1]) >= Number(ports[2])) throw Error('E_INPUT: browser proof port range needs 1 <= A < B <= 65535');
    out.range = [Number(ports[1]), Number(ports[2])];
  }
  return out;
}

   649	## 9. Wave 10: upgrade deltas and visible form proof
   650	
   651	**Implementer:** Codex for Charlie Cheever, 2026-10-13, DX2 commission.
   652	Status of this amendment: Review until both commissioned blind reviews are READY.
   653	
   654	A check remembers its compiler version, schema source identity, emitted API,
   655	note counts by code and refusal counts in `.snapback4/check-surface.json`.
   656	The first check using a different version compares that baseline with the same
   657	project: newly added notes by code, changed generated row/operation types, and
   658	new compiler refusals. If the source changed too, the delta says so; it does not
   659	attribute application edits to the compiler. Missing baseline means explicitly
   660	unavailable comparison. Compiler refusals preserve the last baseline and generated
   661	API, so a refusal cannot erase the useful pre-upgrade record. Normal same-version
   662	checks refresh it after successful check. A server/native qualification failure
   663	can leave regenerated, valid Quarry types while retaining the previous baseline. JSON check output stays one
   664	JSON object; human delta goes to stderr in that mode. Snapshot write failures
   665	are reported and never invalidate valid product types. No store adoption, sync
   666	or authority change is introduced.
   667	
   668	Upgrade takes the old installed CLI's check snapshot before installation, then
   669	invokes the newly installed CLI's check after pinning. It preserves the package
   670	manager's dependency sections and ordinary recovery. A compiler refusal after a
   671	successful install returns nonzero with a message distinguishing installation
   672	from qualification; it never rolls back the packages silently. Module-only backends are projects too: all `snapback/*.q` files qualify through
   673	the same loader; schema.q is not required. Without any Quarry source, upgrade
   674	reports package pins only. Exact apps receive one rebuild pointer:
   675	`bun exact.mjs snapback --rebuild` (HS owns the command and device handshake).
   676	`guide changes` packages the release card; its version is bound to the compiler
   677	version and its factual nullable-count examples are covered by the guide tests.
   678	Nullable numeric aggregate writes offer `EXPR ?? 0` as an authored choice, not
   679	auto-apply: hidden keys remain null, authorized missing count groups return zero.
   680	
   681	The witness uses the declared persona independently of a guest-only sign-in
   682	screen by selecting createApp's normal persona option under the explicit
   683	witness flag. It still verifies the actual viewer before writing. Custom apps
   684	retain their visible persona picker; a missing persona app/picker refuses. No persona
   685	works against serve. Default starter examples use guest sign-in; `--as alice`
   686	on a starter that declares alice also works.
   687	
   688	A visible form proof is opt-in with `--field LABEL --value TEXT --button LABEL`.
   689	It fills the labelled control, presses the labelled button and observes that
   690	click's matching durable mutation through the registered app client. After filling it arms the exact labelled button, and accepts a mutation started
   691	in that button's active Chromium click event (through either client or app).
   692	The handler must submit the write before awaiting other work; perform asynchronous
   693	validation before enabling submit. This scoped proof never attributes background
   694	or input autosave writes to a button. Before clicking it arms a fresh observer: an existing sent status or row cannot satisfy
   695	this proof. It waits for terminal sent and asserts the configured args when
   696	provided; failed or timed-out custody is nonzero. No second SDK mutation is
   697	injected. The ordinary SDK-only witness remains available without these flags.
   698	React Native web uses keyboard events (`pressSequentially`) to settle state;
   699	buttons use their accessible labels, not testID. Registration is flag-gated and
   700	cleanup restores its instrumentation. Starters label their field, render write
   701	status above the list, and supply a favicon.
   702	
   703	Port selection precedence is explicit --port/--web-port, explicit --port-range,
   704	then the complete PORT_LO/PORT_HI environment pair, then existing defaults.
   705	Ranges are validated before writing any files, and every selected port lies in
   706	them. The client URL, backend npm launcher, frontend dev/preview launcher,
   707	README and witness script share those selections. Later runtime URL overrides
   708	remain supported; the init range is a scaffold input, not an authority rule.
   709	HS owns the Vite Node declarations; DX2 adds no parallel type shim.
   710	
   711	Expo's web client and README derive their device-versus-journal choice from the
   712	same compiled sync detection. Its smoke helper uses the schema's password,
   713	guest or persona sign-in (password only on prime, with a witness-supplied test
   714	credential; it never puts a secret in source). The helper is inert in ordinary
   715	launches and other segments and waits for restoration. The native device and
   716	normal identity custody remain unchanged.
   717	
   718	Ordinary durable React `run()` owns an onWrite observer before admission and
   719	waits for sent/failed, independently of read subscribers and mounted effects.
   720	Early settlement cannot be missed and concurrent calls match only their own
   721	saved/minted intent id. The promise releases its observer on terminal response
   722	or thrown admission. Offline it waits while the client lives; closing the client
   723	ends its ability to collect a receipt, so callers keep the App alive until
   724	settlement or use the explicit custody option and saved id. Existing
   725	`{ waitSeconds: 0 }` returns pending custody immediately; no pending result is
   726	relabelled sent. This amends the default hook boundary, not client.mutate,
   727	outbox durability, authority or replay-result availability. `sentResult` and
   728	`otherwiseAll` already exist; the recipe and tests cover null, replay and all
   729	five read meanings. No client card word is added.
   730	
   731	Wire id omission already landed in §8 and is re-tested with the documented
   732	full envelopes. Guides add tested loop writes and projection identity, list
   733	supported formats (handle and url; email remains application validation), spell
   734	out fixed-period UTC epoch anchoring and periodEnd, and carry the asset upload
   735	route. Aliases resolve optional param, mutation default and service seat.
   736	Non-TTY reads print a complete card; explicit section/find still narrows it.
   737	REL2 owns release lifecycle narration, DIAG owns general diagnostics and PAGE2
   738	owns planner/rules language; DX2's nullable-write guidance changes no semantics.

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Button, SafeAreaView, Text, View } from "react-native";
import { StatusBar } from "expo-status-bar";
import { AppProvider, useAuth, usePersonaApp } from "snapback4/react";
import { openApp, personas } from "./src/client";

export function App() {
  return <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1 }}><StatusBar style="auto" /><Personas /></SafeAreaView>;
}

// Each persona gets its own app (and device store): usePersonaApp opens it on
// choice and closes it on switch and unmount.
function Personas() {
  const [persona, setPersona] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const app = usePersonaApp(persona, openApp);
  if (!persona) return <View style={{ padding: 24, gap: 12 }}>
    <Text style={{ fontSize: 24 }}>Act as</Text>
    {personas.map(name => <Button key={name} title={name} onPress={() => setPersona(name)} />)}
  </View>;
  if (!app) return <Text style={{ padding: 24 }}>Opening…</Text>;
  return <AppProvider app={app}><Account leave={() => setPersona(null)}><Todos /></Account></AppProvider>;
}

// Every read and write runs as the persona; there is no session to restore.
function Account({ leave, children }: { leave: () => void; children: ReactNode }) {
  const auth = useAuth();
  if (auth.state === "signed-in") return children;
  return <View style={{ padding: 24, gap: 12 }}>
    <Text>{auth.state === "restoring" ? "Opening…" : auth.why?.message ?? "Signed out"}</Text>
    <Button title="Choose a persona" onPress={leave} />
  </View>;
}
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Button, SafeAreaView, Text, View } from "react-native";
import { StatusBar } from "expo-status-bar";
import { AppProvider, useAuth, usePersonaApp } from "snapback4/react";
import { smokeSegment } from "snapback4-expo/witness";
import { openApp, personas } from "./src/client";

export function App() {
  return <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1 }}><StatusBar style="auto" /><Personas /></SafeAreaView>;
}

// Each persona gets its own app (and device store): usePersonaApp opens it on
// choice and closes it on switch and unmount.
function Personas() {
  const [persona, setPersona] = useState<string | null>(() => smokeSegment() ? personas[0] ?? null : null);
  const app = usePersonaApp(persona, openApp);
  if (!persona) return <View style={{ padding: 24, gap: 12 }}>
    <Text style={{ fontSize: 24 }}>Act as</Text>
    {personas.map(name => <Button key={name} title={name} onPress={() => setPersona(name)} />)}
  </View>;
  if (!app) return <Text style={{ padding: 24 }}>Opening…</Text>;
  return <AppProvider app={app}><Account leave={() => setPersona(null)}><Todos /></Account></AppProvider>;
}

// Every read and write runs as the persona; there is no session to restore.
function Account({ leave, children }: { leave: () => void; children: ReactNode }) {
  const auth = useAuth();
  if (auth.state === "signed-in") return children;
  return <View style={{ padding: 24, gap: 12 }}>
    <Text>{auth.state === "restoring" ? "Opening…" : auth.why?.message ?? "Signed out"}</Text>
    <Button title="Choose a persona" onPress={leave} />
  </View>;
}
# TITLE

An Expo (React Native) app over the Snapback 4 backend in `snapback/*.q`,
started from the Snapback 4 Expo starter. Development and production builds
open the native device; Expo Go uses the online client; web uses the wasm
device.

OFFLINE

ADVISORIES

```sh
npm install
npx snapback4 check      # compiles snapback/*.q, writes snapback/generated/api.ts
npx snapback4 dev        # serves http://127.0.0.1:4400 and reloads on save
npm start                # in a second terminal: Expo Go, or press w for web
npm run typecheck
```

`npm run ios:dev` builds the native development build. The iOS simulator
reaches http://127.0.0.1:4400 on this machine (`EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL`
overrides it); for a physical phone set `EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL` to your TLS
deployment. `app.json`'s name, slug, scheme and bundle ids are named after this
directory; change the bundle ids before shipping.

SIGN_IN

## Files

- `snapback/*.q`: the backend (tables, rules, `sync`, queries, mutations).
  Edit it and `snapback4 dev` re-adopts it; `snapback4 check` regenerates the API.
- `fixtures/`: your rows as `fixtures/<table>.jsonl` and expectations in
  `fixtures/test.cases.jsonl`, for `npx snapback4 test --fixture fixtures`
  (`npx snapback4 guide testing`).
- `src/client.ts`: one `createApp` per app; it picks the native device, the
  online client (Expo Go) or `webDevice()` (web).
- `App.tsx`: the sign-in and `Home`, a screen to replace. It shows the viewer,
  your operations and your first query without required arguments, rendered
  with `matchRead`. Read with `useQuery(api.op, args)` or
  `usePage(api.list, args)` (a query that takes `c: cursor ?`), write with
  `useMutation(api.op)` (`npx snapback4 guide client`).
- `expo-env.d.ts`: Expo's types, including `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_*`.
- `metro.config.js` and `metro-wasm-transformer.cjs`: keep both; Metro then
  ships the web device's wasm and skips the local `.snapback4/` store.

TOOLCHAIN
## Next steps

- Shapes most apps need (roles, two parties, booking, schedules):
  `npx snapback4 guide recipes`; one screen of forms: `guide cheatsheet`.
- A complete multi-screen example: `npx snapback4 init <dir> --expo --template chat`.
